# Master Timeline

This page is the consolidated chronological index of all dated events across the wiki.
Each entry links back to the specific wiki page and section where the event is documented.

## Tier System

- **Tier 1 (Major):** Life events - births, deaths, marriages, divorces, moves, arrests, hospitalizations, job changes, relationship starts/ends, educational milestones, financial crises, legal events
- **Tier 2 (Significant):** Concerts, trips, promotions, purchases, celebrations, health events, social milestones
- **Tier 3 (Notable):** Minor events, recurring activities, contextual references (only from timeline pages)
